| 2010-03-30 | Obama signs the Health Care and Education Reconciliation Act, which amends the new law. — [Affordable Care Act](../articles/contemporary-america/affordable-care-act.md) |
| 2010-04-20 – 2010-09-19 | **[Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md)** (Event) |
| 2010-04-20 | The Deepwater Horizon blowout in the Gulf of Mexico kills 11 workers. — [Bureau of Ocean Energy Management](../articles/geography-and-environment/bureau-of-ocean-energy-management.md) |
| 2010-04-20 | The Macondo well blows out beneath the Deepwater Horizon rig; an explosion and fire kill 11 workers and the blowout preventer fails to activate. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-04-22 | The burning rig sinks in about 5,000 feet of water; aerial dispersant spraying begins the same day. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-04-24 | Officials announce a flow estimate of up to 1,000 barrels a day, raised to 5,000 barrels a day four days later. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-04-28 | The first controlled burn of floating oil and the first fishery closures in state waters. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-05-19 | Secretary Ken Salazar signs Secretarial Order 3299, dividing the Minerals Management Service into three organizations. — [Bureau of Ocean Energy Management](../articles/geography-and-environment/bureau-of-ocean-energy-management.md) |
| 2010-05-22 | President Barack Obama announces a national commission to investigate the spill. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-05-26 | The "top kill" attempt to plug the well begins; it fails by May 28. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-05-30 | A six-month moratorium on deepwater drilling takes effect. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-06-18 | Secretarial Order 3302 renames the agency the Bureau of Ocean Energy Management, Regulation and Enforcement (BOEMRE). — [Bureau of Ocean Energy Management](../articles/geography-and-environment/bureau-of-ocean-energy-management.md) |
| 2010-06-21 | Fishery closures reach about 37 percent of the Gulf of Mexico. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-07-15 | BP closes the valves on a new containment cap and the oil stops flowing. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-07-21 | The Dodd-Frank Wall Street Reform and Consumer Protection Act is signed into law. — [Great Recession](../articles/contemporary-america/great-recession.md) |
| 2010-09-19 | After a relief well intercepts the Macondo well, BP confirms it is permanently sealed with cement. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2010-09-20 | The NBER announces that the recession ended in June 2009. — [Great Recession](../articles/contemporary-america/great-recession.md) |
| 2010-10-01 | Revenue collection moves to a separate Office of Natural Resources Revenue. — [Bureau of Ocean Energy Management](../articles/geography-and-environment/bureau-of-ocean-energy-management.md) |
| 2010-12-29 | The EPA establishes the Chesapeake Bay Total Maximum Daily Load, a cap on nitrogen, phosphorus and sediment. — [Chesapeake Bay](../articles/geography-and-environment/chesapeake-bay.md) |
| 2011 | Researcher Michael Hirsch identifies the last six unidentified victims. — [Triangle Shirtwaist Factory fire](../articles/economy-and-work/triangle-shirtwaist-factory-fire.md) |
| 2011 | The Financial Crisis Inquiry Commission issues its final report, with two dissents. — [Great Recession](../articles/contemporary-america/great-recession.md) |
| 2011-01-11 | The presidential commission releases its final report, Deep Water. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2011-05-02 | Navy SEALs kill Osama bin Laden at a compound near Abbottabad, Pakistan. — [War in Afghanistan (2001–2021)](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/war-in-afghanistan.md) |
| 2011-07 | Afghan forces begin taking over security responsibility from international troops. — [War in Afghanistan (2001–2021)](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/war-in-afghanistan.md) |
| 2011-09-12 | The National September 11 Memorial opens to the public, a day after its dedication on the tenth anniversary. — [September 11 attacks](../articles/contemporary-america/september-11-attacks.md) |
| 2011-10-01 | **[Bureau of Ocean Energy Management](../articles/geography-and-environment/bureau-of-ocean-energy-management.md)** (Institution) |
| 2011-10-01 | BOEM and the Bureau of Safety and Environmental Enforcement are established as separate bureaus; Tommy Beaudreau becomes BOEM's director. — [Bureau of Ocean Energy Management](../articles/geography-and-environment/bureau-of-ocean-energy-management.md) |
| 2012 | Following a 2011 Senate resolution, the House unanimously adopts a resolution condemning the exclusion laws. — [Chinese Exclusion Act](../articles/gilded-age-and-progressive-era/chinese-exclusion-act.md) |
| 2012-06-28 | In NFIB v. Sebelius the Supreme Court upholds the individual mandate as a tax but makes the Medicaid expansion optional for states. — [Affordable Care Act](../articles/contemporary-america/affordable-care-act.md) |
| 2013 | The Supreme Court strikes down a key federal oversight provision of the Voting Rights Act. — [Fifteenth Amendment](../articles/democracy-and-elections/fifteenth-amendment.md) |
| 2013 | United States v. Windsor requires the federal government to treat lawful same-sex marriages equally; dozens of federal courts then strike down state bans. — [Obergefell v. Hodges](../articles/contemporary-america/obergefell-v-hodges.md) |
| 2013-01-29 | BP Exploration and Production is sentenced to $4 billion in criminal fines and penalties after pleading guilty. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2013-02-27 | The Supreme Court hears oral argument after the district court and the D.C. Circuit uphold the Act. — [Shelby County v. Holder](../articles/democracy-and-elections/shelby-county-v-holder.md) |
| 2013-05-01 | The Smithsonian, Colonial Williamsburg and Preservation Virginia announce forensic evidence of survival cannibalism on the remains of a girl called Jane. — [Starving Time of 1609–1610](../articles/colonial-and-revolutionary/starving-time.md) |
| 2013-06-11 | Archaeologists announce forensic evidence of survival cannibalism during the Starving Time. — [Jamestown Colony](../articles/colonial-and-revolutionary/jamestown.md) |
| 2013-06-25 | **[Shelby County v. Holder](../articles/democracy-and-elections/shelby-county-v-holder.md)** (CourtCase) |
| 2013-06-25 | In Shelby County v. Holder the Supreme Court, 5 to 4, holds the Section 4(b) coverage formula unconstitutional, halting preclearance. — [Voting Rights Act of 1965](../articles/democracy-and-elections/voting-rights-act-of-1965.md) |
| 2013-06-25 | The Court rules 5 to 4 that the Section 4(b) coverage formula is unconstitutional. — [Shelby County v. Holder](../articles/democracy-and-elections/shelby-county-v-holder.md) |
| 2013-07-18 | Detroit files for Chapter 9 bankruptcy, the largest municipal filing in U.S. history. — [Detroit](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/detroit.md) |
| 2014 | A second, expanded edition of Island is published by the University of Washington Press. — [Chinese detention-barracks poetry](../articles/culture-and-arts/chinese-detention-barracks-poetry.md) |
| 2014 | The individual mandate, the exchanges with their premium tax credits and the ban on preexisting-condition exclusions for all ages take effect. — [Affordable Care Act](../articles/contemporary-america/affordable-care-act.md) |
| 2014-03-23 | A pulse flow under Minute 319 of the 1944 treaty begins; it eventually reaches the river's estuary in the Gulf of California. — [Colorado River](../articles/geography-and-environment/colorado-river.md) |
| 2014-06-16 | The Chesapeake Bay Watershed Agreement is signed, with 10 goals and 31 outcomes. — [Chesapeake Bay](../articles/geography-and-environment/chesapeake-bay.md) |
| 2014-11 | A divided Sixth Circuit panel upholds the bans in Michigan, Ohio, Kentucky and Tennessee, reversing the trial courts. — [Obergefell v. Hodges](../articles/contemporary-america/obergefell-v-hodges.md) |
| 2014-11-07 | Judge Steven Rhodes confirms the city's plan to exit bankruptcy. — [Detroit](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/detroit.md) |
| 2015 | Eric Foner's Gateway to Freedom documents New York's interracial vigilance committees. — [Underground Railroad](../articles/civil-war-and-reconstruction/underground-railroad.md) |
| 2015 | Obergefell v. Hodges holds that state bans on same-sex marriage violate the clause. — [Equal protection](../articles/rights-and-law/equal-protection.md) |
| 2015-01 | The Supreme Court agrees to hear the four consolidated cases. — [Obergefell v. Hodges](../articles/contemporary-america/obergefell-v-hodges.md) |
| 2015-01-01 | ISAF's combat mission having ended in late 2014, NATO's smaller train-and-advise Resolute Support mission begins. — [War in Afghanistan (2001–2021)](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/war-in-afghanistan.md) |
| 2015-04-28 | The case is argued before the Supreme Court. — [Obergefell v. Hodges](../articles/contemporary-america/obergefell-v-hodges.md) |
| 2015-06-25 | In King v. Burwell the Court, 6 to 3, holds that tax credits are available in states that use the federal exchange. — [Affordable Care Act](../articles/contemporary-america/affordable-care-act.md) |
| 2015-06-26 | **[Obergefell v. Hodges](../articles/contemporary-america/obergefell-v-hodges.md)** (CourtCase) |
| 2015-06-26 | The Court rules 5–4 that states must license and recognize same-sex marriages, overruling Baker v. Nelson. — [Obergefell v. Hodges](../articles/contemporary-america/obergefell-v-hodges.md) |
| 2015-10-05 | The Justice Department announces a $20.8 billion civil settlement with BP, the United States and five Gulf states. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2015-11-18 | The national NAGPRA Review Committee unanimously finds the Osage culturally affiliated with the Mississippian people. — [Cahokia](../articles/native-nations/cahokia.md) |
| 2016 | Evenwel v. Abbott holds that states may draw legislative districts using total population. — [Reynolds v. Sims](../articles/democracy-and-elections/reynolds-v-sims.md) |
| 2016-01 | Obama vetoes the first repeal bill Congress sends him. — [Affordable Care Act](../articles/contemporary-america/affordable-care-act.md) |
| 2016-04-04 | A federal court enters the consent decree, including up to $8.8 billion for natural resource damages. — [Deepwater Horizon oil spill](../articles/contemporary-america/deepwater-horizon-oil-spill.md) |
| 2016-05-09 | The National Bison Legacy Act makes the North American bison the national mammal. — [National symbols of the United States](../articles/symbols-and-national-identity/national-symbols-of-the-united-states.md) |
| 2016-06-26 | Panama's expanded canal opens with new locks on both coasts. — [Third Locks Project](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/third-locks-project.md) |
| 2016-06-26 | The inaugural transit of the expanded canal begins at the new Agua Clara Locks on the Atlantic side. — [Gatun Locks](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/gatun-locks.md) |
| 2018-09-12 | The U.S. Commission on Civil Rights unanimously recommends that Congress restore preclearance. — [Shelby County v. Holder](../articles/democracy-and-elections/shelby-county-v-holder.md) |
| 2018-11-16 | The Cybersecurity and Infrastructure Security Agency Act creates CISA within the department. — [Department of Homeland Security](../articles/contemporary-america/department-of-homeland-security.md) |
| 2018-12-20 | Congress awards a collective Congressional Gold Medal to Chinese American veterans of World War II. — [Chinese Americans](../articles/society-and-people/chinese-americans.md) |
| 2019 | The penalty for going without coverage falls to $0 under the 2017 tax law. — [Affordable Care Act](../articles/contemporary-america/affordable-care-act.md) |
| 2019-06-18 | Governor Gavin Newsom formally apologizes on behalf of the State of California to California Native peoples. — [California Gold Rush](../articles/early-republic-and-expansion/california-gold-rush.md) |
| 2019-08-01 | IEEE dedicates a milestone plaque at Lick Observatory for the 1969 experiment. — [Lunar Laser Ranging Experiment](../articles/science-and-innovation/lunar-laser-ranging-experiment.md) |
| 2020 | McGirt v. Oklahoma holds that only Congress, by clear statement, can disestablish a reservation. — [Tribal sovereignty](../articles/native-nations/tribal-sovereignty.md) |
| 2020 | The census counts 2,746,388 residents, third behind New York City and Los Angeles. — [Chicago](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/chicago.md) |
| 2020-01-20 – 2023-05-11 | **[COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md)** (Event) |
| 2020-01-20 | CDC reports the first laboratory-confirmed U.S. case, in Washington state, and activates its Emergency Operations Center. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-01-31 | HHS Secretary Alex Azar declares a public health emergency; WHO declares a public health emergency of international concern the same day. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-02-29 | The United States and the Taliban sign the Agreement for Bringing Peace to Afghanistan in Doha, Qatar, setting a 14-month withdrawal timeline. — [War in Afghanistan (2001–2021)](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/war-in-afghanistan.md) |
| 2020-02-29 | The first U.S. death from laboratory-confirmed COVID-19 is reported in Washington state. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-03 | Visitor access to the plaza closes during the COVID-19 pandemic. — [Winged Figures of the Republic](../articles/culture-and-arts/winged-figures-of-the-republic.md) |
| 2020-03-11 | WHO declares COVID-19 a pandemic. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-03-13 | The Trump administration declares a nationwide emergency. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-03-27 | The CARES Act (Public Law 116-136) is approved. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The 2020 census counts 3.7 million people who identify as American Indian and Alaska Native alone and 9.7 million alone or in combination. — [Native Americans](../articles/native-nations/native-americans.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The 2020 census counts 383,997 residents. — [New Orleans](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/new-orleans.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The 2020 census counts 46.9 million people who identify as Black alone or in combination with another race. — [African Americans](../articles/society-and-people/african-americans.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The 2020 census counts 78,588,572 people in the West region. — [The West](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/the-west.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The 2020 census counts about 5.2 million people who identify as Chinese (except Taiwanese) alone or in any combination. — [Chinese Americans](../articles/society-and-people/chinese-americans.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The census counts 1,455,271 residents, 7.0 percent more than in 2010. — [Hawaii](../articles/states-and-territories/hawaii.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The census counts 1,603,797 residents. — [Philadelphia](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/philadelphia.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The census counts 13,002,700 residents. — [Pennsylvania](../articles/states-and-territories/pennsylvania.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The census counts 331,449,281 residents, up 7.4 percent from 2010. — [United States](../articles/states-and-territories/united-states.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The census counts 39,538,223 residents; California remains the most populous state but loses one House seat. — [California](../articles/states-and-territories/california.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The census counts 576,851 residents, the fewest of any state. — [Wyoming](../articles/states-and-territories/wyoming.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The census counts 639,111 residents. — [Detroit](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/detroit.md) |
| 2020-04-01 | The census counts 733,391 residents. — [Alaska](../articles/states-and-territories/alaska.md) |
| 2020-04-30 | The administration launches Operation Warp Speed to speed development of a vaccine. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-05-08 | BLS reports April unemployment of 14.7 percent and a loss of 20.5 million payroll jobs. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-05-28 | Recorded U.S. COVID-19 deaths pass 100,000. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-07-06 | In Chiafalo v. Washington, the Supreme Court rules that states may enforce electors' pledges to vote for their party's nominee. — [Electoral College](../articles/democracy-and-elections/electoral-college.md) |
| 2020-08-07 | A federal court in New York terminates the Paramount consent decrees after more than seventy years. — [Hollywood](../articles/culture-and-arts/hollywood.md) |
| 2020-12-11 | FDA issues the first COVID-19 vaccine emergency use authorization, for the Pfizer-BioNTech vaccine. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2020-12-14 | New York nurse Sandra Lindsay becomes the first American outside clinical trials to receive a COVID-19 vaccine. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2021 | The Smithsonian releases the Smithsonian Anthology of Hip-Hop and Rap, 129 tracks on nine CDs. — [Hip hop](../articles/culture-and-arts/hip-hop.md) |
| 2021-04-08 | A study in Geoarchaeology reports no physical evidence for the long-accepted theory that deforestation caused floods that doomed Cahokia. — [Cahokia](../articles/native-nations/cahokia.md) |
| 2021-04-14 | President Biden announces a final withdrawal to begin May 1, later setting August 31 as the end of the military mission. — [War in Afghanistan (2001–2021)](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/war-in-afghanistan.md) |
| 2021-04-26 | The Census Bureau delivers 2020 apportionment counts; Texas gains two seats and seven states lose one. — [Census and apportionment](../articles/democracy-and-elections/census-and-apportionment.md) |
| 2021-06 | The City Council votes to rename Lake Shore Drive in honor of DuSable. — [Chicago](../articles/regions-cities-and-landmarks/chicago.md) |
| 2021-06-01 | The Delta variant becomes dominant in the United States. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2021-06-17 | In California v. Texas the Court, 7 to 2, dismisses a challenge to the law for lack of standing. — [Affordable Care Act](../articles/contemporary-america/affordable-care-act.md) |
| 2021-08-15 | The Taliban enter Kabul and President Ashraf Ghani flees the country. — [War in Afghanistan (2001–2021)](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/war-in-afghanistan.md) |
| 2021-08-16 | The Bureau of Reclamation declares the first shortage in Lake Mead's history, cutting 2022 deliveries to Arizona, Nevada and Mexico. — [Colorado River](../articles/geography-and-environment/colorado-river.md) |
| 2021-08-26 | A suicide bombing claimed by the Islamic State affiliate at a Kabul airport gate kills 13 U.S. service members and dozens of Afghans. — [War in Afghanistan (2001–2021)](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/war-in-afghanistan.md) |
| 2021-08-30 | The last U.S. forces leave Kabul, ending the U.S. military and diplomatic withdrawal. — [War in Afghanistan (2001–2021)](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/war-in-afghanistan.md) |
| 2021-11 | The Senate fails to invoke cloture on the John Lewis Voting Rights Advancement Act, which the House had passed. — [Shelby County v. Holder](../articles/democracy-and-elections/shelby-county-v-holder.md) |
| 2021-12-01 | The first U.S. case of the Omicron variant is detected in California. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2022 | Reclamation contractors begin a full restoration; the figures are sandblasted and repatinated and the old star map is demolished to install drainage. — [Winged Figures of the Republic](../articles/culture-and-arts/winged-figures-of-the-republic.md) |
| 2022 | The Angel Island Immigration Museum opens in the restored hospital building. — [Angel Island Immigration Station](../articles/society-and-people/angel-island-immigration-station.md) |
| 2022-01-05 | Louisiana Governor John Bel Edwards grants Plessy a posthumous pardon. — [Plessy v. Ferguson](../articles/gilded-age-and-progressive-era/plessy-v-ferguson.md) |
| 2022-05-12 | Recorded U.S. COVID-19 deaths reach 1 million. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2022-11 | Funding ends for the Interior Department's Land Buy-Back Program, which restored nearly 3 million fractionated acres to tribal trust ownership. — [Dawes Act](../articles/native-nations/dawes-act.md) |
| 2022-12-13 | The Respect for Marriage Act is signed into law, repealing the Defense of Marriage Act's marriage definition and requiring interstate recognition of marriages. — [Obergefell v. Hodges](../articles/contemporary-america/obergefell-v-hodges.md) |
| 2023-05-11 | The federal COVID-19 public health emergency expires at the end of the day. — [COVID-19 pandemic in the United States](../articles/contemporary-america/covid-19-pandemic-in-the-united-states.md) |
| 2023-07 | Preservationists launch a Save the Star Map campaign after the terrazzo work stalls. — [Winged Figures of the Republic](../articles/culture-and-arts/winged-figures-of-the-republic.md) |
| 2023-07-20 | The U.S. Senate agrees to S.Res. 305, designating August 11, 2023, as Hip Hop Celebration Day for the culture's 50th anniversary. — [Hip hop](../articles/culture-and-arts/hip-hop.md) |
| 2024-03-07 | Sweden becomes the 32nd member. — [North Atlantic Treaty Organization](../articles/foreign-relations-and-military/nato.md) |
| 2024-04-13 | The Cherokee Nation includes chunkey in a Traditional Native Games competition at One Fire Field in Tahlequah, Oklahoma. — [Chunkey](../articles/native-nations/chunkey.md) |
| 2024-07-30 | The Interior Department's boarding school report identifies 417 federal Indian boarding schools and at least 973 children who died while attending them. — [Native Americans](../articles/native-nations/native-americans.md) |
| 2024-12-23 | Public Law 118-206 designates the bald eagle as the national bird. — [National symbols of the United States](../articles/symbols-and-national-identity/national-symbols-of-the-united-states.md) |
| 2025 | The BLS counts 14.7 million union members, 10.0 percent of wage and salary workers. — [Labor movement](../articles/economy-and-work/labor-movement.md) |
| 2025-11-10 | The Supreme Court denies review in Davis v. Ermold, a former Kentucky clerk's petition that had asked it to revisit Obergefell. — [Obergefell v. Hodges](../articles/contemporary-america/obergefell-v-hodges.md) |
| 2025-12-02 | The Chesapeake Executive Council approves a revised agreement setting most goals for 2040 after regional 2025 targets are missed. — [Chesapeake Bay](../articles/geography-and-environment/chesapeake-bay.md) |
| 2026-04-03 | The Interior Department announces plans to merge BOEM and BSEE into a Marine Minerals Administration. — [Bureau of Ocean Energy Management](../articles/geography-and-environment/bureau-of-ocean-energy-management.md) |
| 2026-04-29 | In Louisiana v. Callais the Court narrows Section 2, the Act's nationwide ban, in redistricting cases. — [Shelby County v. Holder](../articles/democracy-and-elections/shelby-county-v-holder.md) |
| 2026-04-29 | In Louisiana v. Callais the Supreme Court, 6 to 3, holds that Section 2 imposes liability in redistricting only where evidence supports a strong inference of intentional discrimination. — [Voting Rights Act of 1965](../articles/democracy-and-elections/voting-rights-act-of-1965.md) |
| 2026-07-10 | Secretary's Order 3451 establishes the Marine Minerals Administration and vests BOEM's functions in it, with a phased transition. — [Bureau of Ocean Energy Management](../articles/geography-and-environment/bureau-of-ocean-energy-management.md) |
